# KTM 250 XC-F 2026: aftermarket parts

4300 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 30 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 3259 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 1011 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
